Paṭisambhidāmagga Paṭisambhidāmagga

1 Mahāvagga 1 Mahāvagga

1.1. Ñāṇakathā 1.1. On Knowledge

1.1.1. Sutamayañāṇaniddesa 1.1.1. Chapter on Learning

Kathaṁ sotāvadhāne paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ? How is it that understanding of applying the ear is knowledge of what consists in the heard (learnt)?

“Ime dhammā abhiññeyyā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. The ear is applied thus: “These ideas are to be directly-known“. Understanding as the act of understanding that is knowledge of what consists in the heard (learnt).

“Ime dhammā pariññeyyā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “These ideas are to be fully-understood” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Ime dhammā pahātabbā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “These ideas are to be abandoned.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Ime dhammā bhāvetabbā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “These ideas are to be developed.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Ime dhammā sacchikātabbā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “These ideas are to be realized.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Ime dhammā hānabhāgiyā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “These ideas partake of diminution.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Ime dhammā ṭhitibhāgiyā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “These ideas partake of stagnation.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Ime dhammā visesabhāgiyā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “These ideas partake of distinction.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Ime dhammā nibbedhabhāgiyā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “ideas partake of penetration.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Sabbe saṅkhārā aniccā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “All formations are impermanent.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Sabbe saṅkhārā dukkhā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “All formations are painful.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Sabbe dhammā anattā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “All formations are not-self.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Idaṁ dukkhaṁ ariyasaccan”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “This is the noble actuality of suffering.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Idaṁ dukkhasamudayaṁ ariyasaccan”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “This is the noble actuality of the origin of suffering.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Idaṁ dukkhanirodhaṁ ariyasaccan”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “This is the noble actuality of the cessation of suffering.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

“Idaṁ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccan”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “This is the noble actuality of the way leading to the cessation of suffering.” The ear is applied thus. Understanding...

Kathaṁ “ime dhammā abhiññeyyā”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ? How is it that understanding of applying the ear thus “These ideas are to be directly-known” is knowledge of what consists in the heard (learnt}?

Eko dhammo abhiññeyyo—One idea to be directly known—

sabbe sattā āhāraṭṭhitikā. All beings are maintained by nutriment..

Dve dhammā abhiññeyyā—Two ideas to be directly known—

dve dhātuyo. Two principles [the formed and unformed]

Tayo dhammā abhiññeyyā—Three ideas to be directly known—

tisso dhātuyo. Three principles [those of sense desire, of the material, and of the immaterial]

Cattāro dhammā abhiññeyyā—Four ideas are be directly known:

cattāri ariyasaccāni. The four noble actualities.

Pañca dhammā abhiññeyyā—Five ideas to be directly known:

pañca vimuttāyatanāni. Five bases for deliverance.

Cha dhammā abhiññeyyā—Six ideas to be directly known:

cha anuttariyāni. Six unsurpassables.

Satta dhammā abhiññeyyā—Seven ideas to be directly known:

satta niddasavatthūni. Seven grounds for commendation

Aṭṭha dhammā abhiññeyyā—Eight ideas to be directly known:

aṭṭha abhibhāyatanāni. Eight bases of mastery.

Nava dhammā abhiññeyyā—Nine ideas to be directly known:

nava anupubbavihārā. Nine successive abidings.

Dasa dhammā abhiññeyyā—Ten Ideas to be directly known:

dasa nijjaravatthūni. [10] Ten grounds for decay. [10]

“Sabbaṁ, bhikkhave, abhiññeyyaṁ. “Bhikkhus. All is to be directly known.

Kiñca, bhikkhave, sabbaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ? And what is all that is to be directly known?

Cakkhu, bhikkhave, abhiññeyyaṁ; Eye is to be directly known;

rūpā abhiññeyyā; visible objects are to be directly known;

cakkhuviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; eye consciousness is to be directly known;

cakkhusamphasso abhiññeyyo; eye contact is to be directly known;

yampidaṁ cakkhusamphassapaccayā uppajjati vedayitaṁ sukhaṁ vā dukkhaṁ vā adukkhamasukhaṁ vā, tampi abhiññeyyaṁ. any feeling that arises with eye contact as its condition whether pleasant or painful or neither-painful-nor-pleasant is also to be directly known.

Sotaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Ear is to be directly known;

saddā abhiññeyyā … Sounds ...

ghānaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Nose is to be directly known;

gandhā abhiññeyyā … Odours ...

jivhā abhiññeyyā; Tongue is to be directly known;

rasā abhiññeyyā … Flavours ...

kāyo abhiññeyyo; Body is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbā abhiññeyyā … Tangible objects ...

mano abhiññeyyo; Mind is to be directly known;

dhammā abhiññeyyā; Ideas are lo be directly known;

manoviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ, mind consciousness is to be directly known;

manosamphasso abhiññeyyo; mind contact is to be directly known;

yampidaṁ manosamphassapaccayā uppajjati vedayitaṁ sukhaṁ vā dukkhaṁ vā adukkhamasukhaṁ vā, tampi abhiññeyyaṁ”. [30] any feeling that arises with mind contact as us condition whether pleasant or painful or neither-painful-nor-pleasant is also to be directly known”. [30]

Rūpaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Materiality is to be directly known;

vedanā abhiññeyyā; Feeling is to be directly known;

saññā abhiññeyyā; Perception is to be directly known;

saṅkhārā abhiññeyyā; Formations is to be directly known;

viññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. [5] Consciousness is to be directly known. [5]

Cakkhu abhiññeyyaṁ; Eye is to be directly known;

sotaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Ear is to be directly known;

ghānaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Nose is to be directly known;

jivhā abhiññeyyā; Tongue is to be directly known;

kāyo abhiññeyyo; Body is to be directly known;

mano abhiññeyyo. Mind is to be directly known.

Rūpā abhiññeyyā; Visible objects is to be directly known;

saddā abhiññeyyā; Sounds is to be directly known;

gandhā abhiññeyyā; Odours is to be directly known;

rasā abhiññeyyā; Flavours is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbā abhiññeyyā; Tangible Objects is to be directly known;

dhammā abhiññeyyā. Ideas is to be directly known.

Cakkhuviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Eye-consciousness is to be directly known;

sotaviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Ear-consciousness is to be directly known;

ghānaviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Nose-consciousness is to be directly known;

jivhāviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Tongue-consciousness is to be directly known;

kāyaviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Body-consciousness is to be directly known;

manoviññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. Mind-consciousness is to be directly known.

Cakkhusamphasso abhiññeyyo; Eye-contact is to be directly known;

sotasamphasso abhiññeyyo; Ear--contact is to be directly known;

ghānasamphasso abhiññeyyo; Nose-contact is to be directly known;

jivhāsamphasso abhiññeyyo; Tongue-contact is to be directly known;

kāyasamphasso abhiññeyyo; Body-contact is to be directly known;

manosamphasso abhiññeyyo; Mind-contact is to be directly known;

cakkhusamphassajā vedanā abhiññeyyā; Eye-contact-born feeling is to be directly known;

sotasamphassajā vedanā abhiññeyyā; Ear-contact-born feeling is to be directly known;

ghānasamphassajā vedanā abhiññeyyā; Nose-contact-born feeling is to be directly known;

jivhāsamphassajā vedanā abhiññeyyā; Tongue-contact-born feeling is to be directly known;

kāyasamphassajā vedanā abhiññeyyā; Body-contact-born feeling is to be directly known;

manosamphassajā vedanā abhiññeyyā. Mind-contact-born feeling is to be directly known.

Rūpasaññā abhiññeyyā; Perception of visible objects is to be directly known;

saddasaññā abhiññeyyā; Perception of sounds is to be directly known;

gandhasaññā abhiññeyyā; Perception of odours is to be directly known;

rasasaññā abhiññeyyā; Perception of flavours is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbasaññā abhiññeyyā; Perception of tangible objects is to be directly known;

dhammasaññā abhiññeyyā. Perception of ideas is to be directly known.

Rūpasañcetanā abhiññeyyā; Volition about visible objects is to be directly known;

saddasañcetanā abhiññeyyā; Volition about sounds is to be directly known;

gandhasañcetanā abhiññeyyā; Volition about odours is to be directly known;

rasasañcetanā abhiññeyyā; Volition about flavours is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbasañcetanā abhiññeyyā; Volition about tangible objects is to be directly known;

dhammasañcetanā abhiññeyyā. Volition about ideas is to be directly known.

Rūpataṇhā abhiññeyyā; Craving for visible objects is to be directly known;

saddataṇhā abhiññeyyā; Craving for sounds is to be directly known;

gandhataṇhā abhiññeyyā; Craving for odours is to be directly known;

rasataṇhā abhiññeyyā; Craving for flavours is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbataṇhā abhiññeyyā; Craving for tangible objects is to be directly known;

dhammataṇhā abhiññeyyā. Craving for ideas is to be directly known.

Rūpavitakko abhiññeyyo; Applied-thought about visible objects is to be directly known;

saddavitakko abhiññeyyo; Applied-thought about sounds is to be directly known;

gandhavitakko abhiññeyyo; Applied-thought about odours is to be directly known;

rasavitakko abhiññeyyo; Applied-thought about flavours is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbavitakko abhiññeyyo; Applied-thought about tangible objects is to be directly known;

dhammavitakko abhiññeyyo. Applied-thought about ideas is to be directly known.

Rūpavicāro abhiññeyyo; Sustained-thought about visible objects is to be directly known;

saddavicāro abhiññeyyo; Sustained-thought about sounds is to be directly known;

gandhavicāro abhiññeyyo; Sustained-thought about odours is to be directly known;

rasavicāro abhiññeyyo; Sustained-thought about flavours is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbavicāro abhiññeyyo; Sustained-thought about tangible objects is to be directly known;

dhammavicāro abhiññeyyo. [60] Sustained-thought about ideas is to be directly known. [60]

Pathavīdhātu abhiññeyyā; The earth principle is to be directly known;

āpodhātu abhiññeyyā; The water principle is to be directly known;

tejodhātu abhiññeyyā; The fire principle is to be directly known;

vāyodhātu abhiññeyyā; The air principle is to be directly known;

ākāsadhātu abhiññeyyā; The space principle is to be directly known;

viññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā. [6] The consciousness principle is to be directly known. [6]

Pathavīkasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The earth kasina is to be directly known;

āpokasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The water kasina is to be directly known;

tejokasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The fire kasina is to be directly known;

vāyokasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The air kasina is to be directly known;

nīlakasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The blue kasina is to be directly known;

pītakasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The yellow kasina is to be directly known;

lohitakasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The red kasina is to be directly known;

odātakasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; odāta kasina is to be directly known;

ākāsakasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The space kasina is to be directly known;

viññāṇakasiṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. [10] The consciousness kasina is to be directly known. [10]

Kesā abhiññeyyā; Head hair is to be directly known;

lomā abhiññeyyā; Body hair is to be directly known;

nakhā abhiññeyyā; Nails is to be directly known;

dantā abhiññeyyā; Teeth is to be directly known;

taco abhiññeyyo, maṁsaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Skin is to be directly known, Flesh is to be directly known;

nhārū abhiññeyyā; Sinew is to be directly known;

aṭṭhī abhiññeyyā; Bones is to be directly known;

aṭṭhimiñjā abhiññeyyā; Marrow is to be directly known;

vakkaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Kidneys is to be directly known;

hadayaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Heart is to be directly known;

yakanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Liver is to be directly known;

kilomakaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Midriff is to be directly known;

pihakaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Spleen is to be directly known;

papphāsaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Lungs is to be directly known;

antaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ antaguṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Bowels is to be directly known; Entrails is to be directly known;

udariyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Gorge is to be directly known;

karīsaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Dung is to be directly known;

pittaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Bile is to be directly known;

semhaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Phlegm is to be directly known;

pubbo abhiññeyyo; Pus is to be directly known;

lohitaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Blood is to be directly known;

sedo abhiññeyyo; Sweat is to be directly known;

medo abhiññeyyo; Fat is to be directly known;

assu abhiññeyyaṁ; Tears is to be directly known;

vasā abhiññeyyā; Grease is to be directly known;

kheḷo abhiññeyyo; Spittle is to be directly known;

siṅghāṇikā abhiññeyyā; Snot is to be directly known;

lasikā abhiññeyyā; Oil-of-the-joints is to be directly known;

muttaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Sweat is to be directly known;

matthaluṅgaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. [32] Brain is to be directly known. [32]

Cakkhāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The eye base is to be directly known;

rūpāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. The visible object base is to be directly known.

Sotāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The ear base is to be directly known;

saddāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. The sound base is to be directly known.

Ghānāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The nose base is to be directly known;

gandhāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. The odours base is to be directly known.

Jivhāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The tongue base is to be directly known;

rasāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. The flavour base is to be directly known.

Kāyāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The body base is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. The tangible object base is to be directly known.

Manāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The body base is to be directly known;

dhammāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. [12] The idea base is to be directly known. [12]

Cakkhudhātu abhiññeyyā; Eye principle is to be directly known;

rūpadhātu abhiññeyyā; The visible-object principle is to be directly known;

cakkhuviññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā. The eye-consciousness principle is to be directly known.

Sotadhātu abhiññeyyā; The ear principle is to be directly known;

saddadhātu abhiññeyyā; The sound principle is to be directly known;

sotaviññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā. The ear-consciousness principle is to be directly known.

Ghānadhātu abhiññeyyā; The nose principle is to be directly known;

gandhadhātu abhiññeyyā; The odour principle is to be directly known;

ghānaviññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā. The nose-consciousness principle is to be directly known.

Jivhādhātu abhiññeyyā; The tongue principle is to be directly known;

rasadhātu abhiññeyyā; The flavour principle is to be directly known;

jivhāviññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā. The tongue-consciousness principle is to be directly known.

Kāyadhātu abhiññeyyā; The body principle is to be directly known;

phoṭṭhabbadhātu abhiññeyyā; The tangible-object principle is to be directly known;

kāyaviññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā. The body-consciousness principle is to be directly known.

Manodhātu abhiññeyyā; The mind principle is to be directly known;

dhammadhātu abhiññeyyā; The idea principle is to be directly known;

manoviññāṇadhātu abhiññeyyā. [18] The mind-consciousness principle is to be directly known. [18]

Cakkhundriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The eye faculty is to be directly known;

sotindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The ear faculty is to be directly known;

ghānindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The nose faculty is to be directly known;

jivhindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The tongue faculty is to be directly known;

kāyindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The body faculty is to be directly known;

manindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The mind faculty is to be directly known;

jīvitindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The life faculty is to be directly known;

itthindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The femininity faculty is to be directly known;

purisindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The masculinity faculty is to be directly known;

sukhindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The (bodily) pleasure faculty is to be directly known;

dukkhindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The (bodily) pain faculty is to be directly known;

somanassindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The (mental) joy faculty is to be directly known;

domanassindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The (mental) grief faculty is to be directly known;

upekkhindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The equanimity faculty is to be directly known;

saddhindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The faith faculty is to be directly known;

vīriyindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The energy faculty is to be directly known;

satindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The mindfulness faculty is to be directly known;

samādhindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The concentration faculty is to be directly known;

paññindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The understanding faculty is to be directly known;

anaññātaññassāmītindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The I shall-come-to-know-the-unknown faculty is to be directly known;

aññindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The final-knowledge faculty is to be directly known;

aññātāvindriyaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. [22] The final-knower faculty is to be directly known. [22]

Kāmadhātu abhiññeyyā; The sensual desire principle principle is to be directly known;

rūpadhātu abhiññeyyā; The material principle is to be directly known;

arūpadhātu abhiññeyyā. The immaterial principle is to be directly known.

Kāmabhavo abhiññeyyo; Sensual desire being is to be directly known;

rūpabhavo abhiññeyyo; Material being is to be directly known;

arūpabhavo abhiññeyyo. Immaterial being is to be directly known.

Saññābhavo abhiññeyyo; Percipient being is to be directly known;

asaññābhavo abhiññeyyo; Non-ercipient being is to be directly known;

nevasaññānāsaññābhavo abhiññeyyo. Neither percipient nor non-percipient being is to be directly known.

Ekavokārabhavo abhiññeyyo; One-constituent being is to be directly known;

catuvokārabhavo abhiññeyyo; Four-constituent being is to be directly known;

pañcavokārabhavo abhiññeyyo. [12] Five-constituent being is to be directly known. [12]

Paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The first jhana is to be directly known;

dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The second jhana is to be directly known;

tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The third jhana is to be directly known;

catutthaṁ jhānaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. The fourth jhana is to be directly known.

Mettācetovimutti abhiññeyyā; The heart (will) deliverance of loving-kindness is to be directly known;

karuṇācetovimutti abhiññeyyā; The heart-deliverance of compassion is to be directly known;

muditācetovimutti abhiññeyyā; The heart-deliverance of sympathetic gladness is to be directly known;

upekkhācetovimutti abhiññeyyā. The heart-deliverance of equanimity is to be directly known.

Ākāsānañcāyatanasamāpatti abhiññeyyā; The attainment of the base consisting of boundless space is to be directly known;

viññāṇañcāyatanasamāpatti abhiññeyyā; The attainment of the base consisting of boundless consciousness is to be directly known;

ākiñcaññāyatanasamāpatti abhiññeyyā; The attainment of the base consisting of nothingness is to be directly known;

nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpatti abhiññeyyā. [12] The attainment of the base consisting of neither perception nor non-perception is to be directly known. [12]

Avijjā abhiññeyyā; Ignorance is to be directly known;

saṅkhārā abhiññeyyā; Formations is to be directly known;

viññāṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Consciousness is to be directly known;

nāmarūpaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Mentality-materiality is to be directly known;

saḷāyatanaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; The six-fold base is to be directly known;

phasso abhiññeyyo; Contact is to be directly known;

vedanā abhiññeyyā; Feeling is to be directly known;

taṇhā abhiññeyyā; Craving is to be directly known;

upādānaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Clinging is to be directly known;

bhavo abhiññeyyo; Being is to be directly known;

jāti abhiññeyyā; Birth is to be directly known;

jarāmaraṇaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ. [12] Aging and death is to be directly known. [12]

Dukkhaṁ abhiññeyyaṁ; Suffering is to be directly known;

dukkhasamudayo abhiññeyyo; The origin of suffering is to be directly known;

“ime dhammā abhiññeyyāti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇan”ti. [1] “ime dhammā is to be directly knownti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇan”ti. [1]

phasso sāsavo upādāniyo. contact sāsavo upādāniyo.

tisso vedanā. tisso vedanā.

pañcupādānakkhandhā. pañcupādānakkhandhā.

Cha dhammā pariññeyyā—

cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni. cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni.

Satta dhammā pariññeyyā—

satta viññāṇaṭṭhitiyo. satta consciousnessṭṭhitiyo.

aṭṭha lokadhammā. aṭṭha lokadhammā.

Nava dhammā pariññeyyā—

Tattha katamo appiyehi sampayogo dukkho? Tattha katamo appiyehi sampayogo dukkho?

Idha yassa te honti aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā rūpā saddā gandhā rasā phoṭṭhabbā, ye vā panassa te honti anatthakāmā ahitakāmā aphāsukāmā ayogakkhemakāmā, yā tehi saddhiṁ saṅgati samāgamo samodhānaṁ missībhāvo—Idha yassa te honti aniṭṭhā akantā amanāpā rūpā Sounds Odours Flavours Tangible Objects, ye vā panassa te honti anatthakāmā ahitakāmā aphāsukāmā ayogakkhemakāmā, yā tehi saddhiṁ saṅgati samāgamo samodhānaṁ missībhāvo—

ayaṁ vuccati appiyehi sampayogo dukkho. ayaṁ vuccati appiyehi sampayogo dukkho.

Tattha katamo piyehi vippayogo dukkho? Tattha katamo piyehi vippayogo dukkho?

Idha yassa te honti iṭṭhā kantā manāpā rūpā saddā gandhā rasā phoṭṭhabbā, ye vā panassa te honti atthakāmā hitakāmā phāsukāmā yogakkhemakāmā mātā vā pitā vā bhātā vā bhaginī vā mittā vā amaccā vā ñātī vā sālohitā vā, yā tehi saddhiṁ asaṅgati asamāgamo asamodhānaṁ amissībhāvo—Idha yassa te honti iṭṭhā kantā manāpā rūpā Sounds Odours Flavours Tangible Objects, ye vā panassa te honti atthakāmā hitakāmā phāsukāmā yogakkhemakāmā mātā vā pitā vā bhātā vā bhaginī vā mittā vā amaccā vā ñātī vā sālohitā vā, yā tehi saddhiṁ asaṅgati asamāgamo asamodhānaṁ amissībhāvo—

ayaṁ vuccati piyehi vippayogo dukkho. ayaṁ vuccati piyehi vippayogo dukkho.

Tattha katamaṁ yampicchaṁ na labhati tampi dukkhaṁ? Tattha katamaṁ yampicchaṁ na labhati tampi dukkhaṁ?

Jātidhammānaṁ sattānaṁ evaṁ icchā uppajjati—

“aho vata mayaṁ na jātidhammā assāma, na ca vata no jāti āgaccheyyā”ti. “aho vata mayaṁ na jātidhammā assāma, na ca vata no birth āgaccheyyā”ti.

Na kho panetaṁ icchāya pattabbaṁ—

idampi yampicchaṁ na labhati tampi dukkhaṁ. idampi yampicchaṁ na labhati tampi dukkhaṁ.

Jarādhammānaṁ sattānaṁ …pe… Jarādhammānaṁ sattānaṁ …pe…

Tattha katamaṁ dukkhanirodhaṁ ariyasaccaṁ? Tattha katamaṁ dukkhanirodhaṁ ariyasaccaṁ?

Yo tassāyeva taṇhāya asesavirāganirodho cāgo paṭinissaggo mutti anālayo, sā kho panesā taṇhā kattha pahīyamānā pahīyati, kattha nirujjhamānā nirujjhati? Cakkhuloke piyarūpaṁ sātarūpaṁ, etthesā taṇhā pahīyamānā pahīyati, ettha nirujjhamānā nirujjhati …pe… Eyeloke piyamateriality sātarūpaṁ, etthesā craving pahīyamānā pahīyati, ettha nirujjhamānā nirujjhati …pe…

Tattha katamaṁ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṁ? Tattha katamaṁ the way leading to the cessation of suffering paṭipadā ariyasaccaṁ?

Ayameva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko maggo, seyyathidaṁ—

sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvācā sammākammanto sammāājīvo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi. sammādiṭṭhi sammāsaṅkappo sammāvācā sammākammanto sammāājīvo sammāvāyāmo sammāsati sammāsamādhi.

Dukkhe ñāṇaṁ, dukkhasamudaye ñāṇaṁ, dukkhanirodhe ñāṇaṁ, dukkhanirodhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ñāṇaṁ—

Tattha katamo sammāvāyāmo? Tattha katamo sammāvāyāmo?

Idha bhikkhu anuppannānaṁ pāpakānaṁ akusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ anuppādāya chandaṁ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṁ ārabhati cittaṁ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṁ pāpakānaṁ akusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ pahānāya …pe… Idha bhikkhu anuppannānaṁ pāpakānaṁ akusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ anuppādāya chandaṁ janeti vāyamati vīriyaṁ ārabhati cittaṁ paggaṇhāti padahati, uppannānaṁ pāpakānaṁ akusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ pahānāya …pe…

anuppannānaṁ kusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ uppādāya …pe… anuppannānaṁ kusalānaṁ dhammānaṁ uppādāya …pe…

Tattha katamā sammāsati? Tattha katamā sammāsati?

Tattha katamo sammāsamādhi? Tattha katamo sammāsamādhi?

Idha bhikkhu vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṁ savicāraṁ vivekajaṁ pītisukhaṁ paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ upasampajja viharati. Idha bhikkhu vivicceva kāmehi vivicca akusalehi dhammehi savitakkaṁ savicāraṁ vivekajaṁ pītisukhaṁ The first jhana upasampajja viharati.

Vitakkavicārānaṁ vūpasamā ajjhattaṁ sampasādanaṁ cetaso ekodibhāvaṁ avitakkaṁ avicāraṁ samādhijaṁ pītisukhaṁ dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ upasampajja viharati. Vitakkavicārānaṁ vūpasamā ajjhattaṁ sampasādanaṁ cetaso ekodibhāvaṁ avitakkaṁ avicāraṁ samādhijaṁ pītisukhaṁ The second jhana upasampajja viharati.

Pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṁvedeti, yaṁ taṁ ariyā ācikkhanti—“upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī”ti tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ upasampajja viharati. Pītiyā ca virāgā upekkhako ca viharati sato ca sampajāno sukhañca kāyena paṭisaṁvedeti, yaṁ taṁ ariyā ācikkhanti—“upekkhako satimā sukhavihārī”ti The third jhana upasampajja viharati.

Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva somanassadomanassānaṁ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṁ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṁ catutthaṁ jhānaṁ upasampajja viharati. Sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbeva somanassadomanassānaṁ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṁ upekkhāsatipārisuddhiṁ The fourth jhana upasampajja viharati.

Ayaṁ vuccati sammāsamādhi. Ayaṁ vuccati sammāsamādhi.

Idaṁ vuccati dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṁ. Idaṁ vuccati the way leading to the cessation of suffering paṭipadā ariyasaccaṁ.

Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

Tena vuccati—

“idaṁ dukkhaṁ ariyasaccaṁ, idaṁ dukkhasamudayaṁ ariyasaccaṁ, idaṁ dukkhanirodhaṁ ariyasaccaṁ, idaṁ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccan”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. “idaṁ suffering ariyasaccaṁ, idaṁ dukkhasamudayaṁ ariyasaccaṁ, idaṁ dukkhanirodhaṁ ariyasaccaṁ, idaṁ the way leading to the cessation of suffering paṭipadā ariyasaccan”ti sotāvadhānaṁ, taṁpajānanā paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ.

Evaṁ sotāvadhāne paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ. Evaṁ sotāvadhāne paññā sutamaye ñāṇaṁ.

Sutamayañāṇaniddeso paṭhamo. Sutamayañāṇaniddeso paṭhamo.

1.1.2. Sīlamayañāṇaniddesa 1.1.2. Sīlamayañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ sutvāna saṁvare paññā sīlamaye ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ sutvāna saṁvare paññā sīlamaye ñāṇaṁ?

Pañca sīlāni—

pariyantapārisuddhisīlaṁ, apariyantapārisuddhisīlaṁ, paripuṇṇapārisuddhisīlaṁ, aparāmaṭṭhapārisuddhisīlaṁ, paṭippassaddhipārisuddhisīlanti. pariyantapārisuddhisīlaṁ, apariyantapārisuddhisīlaṁ, paripuṇṇapārisuddhisīlaṁ, aparāmaṭṭhapārisuddhisīlaṁ, paṭippassaddhipārisuddhisīlanti.

Atthi sīlaṁ pariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ apariyantaṁ. Atthi sīlaṁ pariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ apariyantaṁ.

Tattha katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ pariyantaṁ? Tattha katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ pariyantaṁ?

Idhekacco jīvitahetu jīvitapaccayā jīvitakāraṇā yathāsamādinnaṁ sikkhāpadaṁ vītikkamati—

idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ jīvitapariyantaṁ. idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ jīvitapariyantaṁ.

Evarūpāni sīlāni khaṇḍāni chiddāni sabalāni kammāsāni na bhujissāni na viññuppasatthāni parāmaṭṭhāni asamādhisaṁvattanikāni na avippaṭisāravatthukāni na pāmojjavatthukāni na pītivatthukāni na passaddhivatthukāni na sukhavatthukāni na samādhivatthukāni na yathābhūtañāṇadassanavatthukāni na ekantanibbidāya na virāgāya na nirodhāya na upasamāya na abhiññāya na sambodhāya na nibbānāya saṁvattanti—

idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ pariyantaṁ. idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ pariyantaṁ.

Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ apariyantaṁ? Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ apariyantaṁ?

Atthi sīlaṁ na lābhapariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na yasapariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na ñātipariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na aṅgapariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na jīvitapariyantaṁ. Atthi sīlaṁ na lābhapariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na yasapariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na ñātipariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na aṅgapariyantaṁ, atthi sīlaṁ na jīvitapariyantaṁ.

Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na lābhapariyantaṁ? Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na lābhapariyantaṁ?

Idhekacco lābhahetu lābhapaccayā lābhakāraṇā yathāsamādinnaṁ sikkhāpadaṁ vītikkamāya cittampi na uppādeti, kiṁ so vītikkamissati. Idhekacco lābhahetu lābhapaccayā lābhakāraṇā yathāsamādinnaṁ sikkhāpadaṁ vītikkamāya cittampi na uppādeti, kiṁ so vītikkamissati.

Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na lābhapariyantaṁ. Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na lābhapariyantaṁ.

Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na yasapariyantaṁ? Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na yasapariyantaṁ?

Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na yasapariyantaṁ. Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na yasapariyantaṁ.

Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na ñātipariyantaṁ? Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na ñātipariyantaṁ?

Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na ñātipariyantaṁ. Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na ñātipariyantaṁ.

Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na aṅgapariyantaṁ? Katamaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na aṅgapariyantaṁ?

Idhekacco aṅgahetu aṅgapaccayā aṅgakāraṇā yathāsamādinnaṁ sikkhāpadaṁ vītikkamāya cittampi na uppādeti, kiṁ so vītikkamissati. Idhekacco aṅgahetu aṅgapaccayā aṅgakāraṇā yathāsamādinnaṁ sikkhāpadaṁ vītikkamāya cittampi na uppādeti, kiṁ so vītikkamissati.

Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na aṅgapariyantaṁ. Idaṁ taṁ sīlaṁ na aṅgapariyantaṁ.

1.1.4. Dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaniddesa 1.1.4. Dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ paccayapariggahe paññā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ paccayapariggahe paññā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṁ?

Avijjā saṅkhārānaṁ uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca. Ignorance saṅkhārānaṁ uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca.

Imehi navahākārehi avijjā paccayo, saṅkhārā paccayasamuppannā. Imehi navahākārehi Ignorance paccayo, formations paccayasamuppannā.

Ubhopete dhammā paccayasamuppannāti—

paccayapariggahe paññā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṁ. paccayapariggahe paññā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṁ.

Atītampi addhānaṁ … Atītampi addhānaṁ …

anāgatampi addhānaṁ avijjā saṅkhārānaṁ uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca. anāgatampi addhānaṁ Ignorance saṅkhārānaṁ uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca.

Imehi navahākārehi avijjā paccayo, saṅkhārā paccayasamuppannā. Imehi navahākārehi Ignorance paccayo, formations paccayasamuppannā.

Ubhopete dhammā paccayasamuppannāti—

phasso vedanāya … contact vedanāya …

vedanā taṇhāya … feeling taṇhāya …

taṇhā upādānassa … craving upādānassa …

upādānaṁ bhavassa … clinging bhavassa …

bhavo jātiyā … being jātiyā …

jāti jarāmaraṇassa uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca. birth jarāmaraṇassa uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca.

Imehi navahākārehi jāti paccayo, jarāmaraṇaṁ paccayasamuppannaṁ. Imehi navahākārehi birth paccayo, aging and death paccayasamuppannaṁ.

Ubhopete dhammā paccayasamuppannāti—

paccayapariggahe paññā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṁ. paccayapariggahe paññā dhammaṭṭhitiñāṇaṁ.

anāgatampi addhānaṁ jāti jarāmaraṇassa uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca. anāgatampi addhānaṁ birth jarāmaraṇassa uppādaṭṭhiti ca pavattaṭṭhiti ca nimittaṭṭhiti ca āyūhanaṭṭhiti ca saññogaṭṭhiti ca palibodhaṭṭhiti ca samudayaṭṭhiti ca hetuṭṭhiti ca paccayaṭṭhiti ca.

taṇhā paṭiccā, upādānaṁ paṭiccasamuppannaṁ … craving paṭiccā, clinging paṭiccasamuppannaṁ …

upādānaṁ paṭiccaṁ, bhavo paṭiccasamuppanno … clinging paṭiccaṁ, being paṭiccasamuppanno …

bhavo paṭicco, jāti paṭiccasamuppannā … being paṭicco, birth paṭiccasamuppannā …

jāti paṭiccā, jarāmaraṇaṁ paṭiccasamuppannaṁ. birth paṭiccā, aging and death paṭiccasamuppannaṁ.

Ubhopete dhammā paṭiccasamuppannāti—

1.1.5. Sammasanañāṇaniddesa 1.1.5. Sammasanañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṁ dhammānaṁ saṅkhipitvā vavatthāne paññā sammasane ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ atītānāgatapaccuppannānaṁ dhammānaṁ saṅkhipitvā vavatthāne paññā sammasane ñāṇaṁ?

Yaṁ kiñci rūpaṁ atītānāgatapaccuppannaṁ ajjhattaṁ vā bahiddhā vā oḷārikaṁ vā sukhumaṁ vā hīnaṁ vā paṇītaṁ vā yaṁ dūre santike vā, sabbaṁ rūpaṁ aniccato vavattheti ekaṁ sammasanaṁ, dukkhato vavattheti ekaṁ sammasanaṁ, anattato vavattheti ekaṁ sammasanaṁ. Yaṁ kiñci materiality atītānāgatapaccuppannaṁ ajjhattaṁ vā bahiddhā vā oḷārikaṁ vā sukhumaṁ vā hīnaṁ vā paṇītaṁ vā yaṁ dūre santike vā, sabbaṁ materiality aniccato vavattheti ekaṁ sammasanaṁ, dukkhato vavattheti ekaṁ sammasanaṁ, anattato vavattheti ekaṁ sammasanaṁ.

Yā kāci vedanā …pe… Yā kāci feeling …pe…

yā kāci saññā … yā kāci saññā …

ye keci saṅkhārā … ye keci formations …

Rūpaṁ atītānāgatapaccuppannaṁ aniccaṁ khayaṭṭhena, dukkhaṁ bhayaṭṭhena anattā asārakaṭṭhenāti saṅkhipitvā vavatthāne paññā sammasane ñāṇaṁ. materiality atītānāgatapaccuppannaṁ aniccaṁ khayaṭṭhena, suffering bhayaṭṭhena anattā asārakaṭṭhenāti saṅkhipitvā vavatthāne paññā sammasane ñāṇaṁ.

1.1.6. Udayabbayañāṇaniddesa 1.1.6. Udayabbayañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ paccuppannānaṁ dhammānaṁ vipariṇāmānupassane paññā udayabbayānupassane ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ paccuppannānaṁ dhammānaṁ vipariṇāmānupassane paññā udayabbayānupassane ñāṇaṁ?

Jātaṁ rūpaṁ paccuppannaṁ, tassa nibbattilakkhaṇaṁ udayo, vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṁ vayo, anupassanā ñāṇaṁ. Jātaṁ materiality paccuppannaṁ, tassa nibbattilakkhaṇaṁ udayo, vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṁ vayo, anupassanā ñāṇaṁ.

Pañcannaṁ khandhānaṁ udayaṁ passanto kati lakkhaṇāni passati, vayaṁ passanto kati lakkhaṇāni passati, udayabbayaṁ passanto kati lakkhaṇāni passati? Pañcannaṁ khandhānaṁ udayaṁ passanto kati lakkhaṇāni passati, vayaṁ passanto kati lakkhaṇāni passati, udayabbayaṁ passanto kati lakkhaṇāni passati?

udayabbayaṁ passanto paññāsa lakkhaṇāni passati. udayabbayaṁ passanto paññāsa lakkhaṇāni passati.

Vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati? Vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati?

Avijjāsamudayā vedanāsamudayoti—

paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Taṇhāsamudayā vedanāsamudayoti—

paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Kammasamudayā vedanāsamudayoti—

paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Phassasamudayā vedanāsamudayoti—

paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. paccayasamudayaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Nibbattilakkhaṇaṁ passantopi vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. Nibbattilakkhaṇaṁ passantopi vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati. Vedanākkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati.

Vayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati? Vayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati?

Avijjānirodhā vedanānirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Taṇhānirodhā vedanānirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Kammanirodhā vedanānirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Phassanirodhā vedanānirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṁ passantopi vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. Vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṁ passantopi vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati. Vedanākkhandhassa vayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati.

Udayabbayaṁ passanto imāni dasa lakkhaṇāni passati. Udayabbayaṁ passanto imāni dasa lakkhaṇāni passati.

Saññākkhandhassa …pe… Saññākkhandhassa …pe…

saṅkhārakkhandhassa …pe… saṅkhārakkhandhassa …pe…

viññāṇakkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati? consciousnesskkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati?

Avijjāsamudayā viññāṇasamudayoti—Avijjāsamudayā consciousnesssamudayoti—

paccayasamudayaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. paccayasamudayaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Taṇhāsamudayā viññāṇasamudayoti paccayasamudayaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. Taṇhāsamudayā consciousnesssamudayoti paccayasamudayaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Kammasamudayā viññāṇasamudayoti—Kammasamudayā consciousnesssamudayoti—

paccayasamudayaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. paccayasamudayaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Nāmarūpasamudayā viññāṇasamudayoti—Nāmarūpasamudayā consciousnesssamudayoti—

paccayasamudayaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. paccayasamudayaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Nibbattilakkhaṇaṁ passantopi viññāṇakkhandhassa udayaṁ passati. Nibbattilakkhaṇaṁ passantopi consciousnesskkhandhassa udayaṁ passati.

Viññāṇakkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati. consciousnesskkhandhassa udayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati.

Vayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati? Vayaṁ passanto katamāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati?

Avijjānirodhā viññāṇanirodhoti—Avijjānirodhā consciousnessnirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Taṇhānirodhā viññāṇanirodhoti—Taṇhānirodhā consciousnessnirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Kammanirodhā viññāṇanirodhoti—Kammanirodhā consciousnessnirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Nāmarūpanirodhā viññāṇanirodhoti—Nāmarūpanirodhā consciousnessnirodhoti—

paccayanirodhaṭṭhena viññāṇakkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. paccayanirodhaṭṭhena consciousnesskkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṁ passantopi viññāṇakkhandhassa vayaṁ passati. Vipariṇāmalakkhaṇaṁ passantopi consciousnesskkhandhassa vayaṁ passati.

Viññāṇakkhandhassa vayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati. consciousnesskkhandhassa vayaṁ passanto imāni pañca lakkhaṇāni passati.

Udayabbayaṁ passanto imāni dasa lakkhaṇāni passati. Udayabbayaṁ passanto imāni dasa lakkhaṇāni passati.

Pañcannaṁ khandhānaṁ udayaṁ passanto imāni pañcavīsati lakkhaṇāni passati, vayaṁ passanto imāni pañcavīsati lakkhaṇāni passati, udayabbayaṁ passanto imāni paññāsa lakkhaṇāni passati. Pañcannaṁ khandhānaṁ udayaṁ passanto imāni pañcavīsati lakkhaṇāni passati, vayaṁ passanto imāni pañcavīsati lakkhaṇāni passati, udayabbayaṁ passanto imāni paññāsa lakkhaṇāni passati.

Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

Tena vuccati—

“paccuppannānaṁ dhammānaṁ vipariṇāmānupassane paññā udayabbayānupassane ñāṇaṁ”. “paccuppannānaṁ dhammānaṁ vipariṇāmānupassane paññā udayabbayānupassane ñāṇaṁ”.

Rūpakkhandho āhārasamudayo. Rūpakkhandho āhārasamudayo.

Vedanā, saññā, saṅkhārā—

tayo khandhā phassasamudayā. tayo khandhā phassasamudayā.

Viññāṇakkhandho nāmarūpasamudayo. consciousnesskkhandho nāmarūpasamudayo.

Udayabbayañāṇaniddeso chaṭṭho. Udayabbayañāṇaniddeso chaṭṭho.

1.1.7. Bhaṅgānupassanāñāṇaniddesa 1.1.7. Bhaṅgānupassanāñāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ ārammaṇaṁ paṭisaṅkhā bhaṅgānupassane paññā vipassane ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ ārammaṇaṁ paṭisaṅkhā bhaṅgānupassane paññā vipassane ñāṇaṁ?

Rūpārammaṇatā cittaṁ uppajjitvā bhijjati. Rūpārammaṇatā cittaṁ uppajjitvā bhijjati.

Taṁ ārammaṇaṁ paṭisaṅkhā tassa cittassa bhaṅgaṁ anupassati. Taṁ ārammaṇaṁ paṭisaṅkhā tassa cittassa bhaṅgaṁ anupassati.

Anupassatīti, kathaṁ anupassati? Anupassatīti, kathaṁ anupassati?

Aniccato anupassati, no niccato. Aniccato anupassati, no niccato.

Dukkhato anupassati, no sukhato. Dukkhato anupassati, no sukhato.

Anattato anupassati, no attato. Anattato anupassati, no attato.

Nibbindati, no nandati, virajjati, no rajjati. Nibbindati, no nandati, virajjati, no rajjati.

Nirodheti, no samudeti. Nirodheti, no samudeti.

Paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati. Paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati.

Aniccato anupassanto niccasaññaṁ pajahati. Aniccato anupassanto niccasaññaṁ pajahati.

Nibbindanto nandiṁ pajahati. Nibbindanto nandiṁ pajahati.

Virajjanto rāgaṁ pajahati. Virajjanto rāgaṁ pajahati.

paṭisaṅkhā vipassanā. paṭisaṅkhā vipassanā.

Ārammaṇaanvayena, Ārammaṇaanvayena,

vayalakkhaṇavipassanā. vayalakkhaṇavipassanā.

1.1.9. Saṅkhārupekkhāñāṇaniddesa 1.1.9. Saṅkhārupekkhāñāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ?

Uppādaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ, pavattaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ, nimittaṁ muñcitukamyatā …pe… Uppādaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ, pavattaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ, nimittaṁ muñcitukamyatā …pe…

āyūhanaṁ muñcitukamyatā … āyūhanaṁ muñcitukamyatā …

paṭisandhiṁ muñcitukamyatā … paṭisandhiṁ muñcitukamyatā …

gatiṁ muñcitukamyatā … gatiṁ muñcitukamyatā …

nibbattiṁ muñcitukamyatā … nibbattiṁ muñcitukamyatā …

upapattiṁ muñcitukamyatā … upapattiṁ muñcitukamyatā …

jātiṁ muñcitukamyatā … jātiṁ muñcitukamyatā …

jaraṁ muñcitukamyatā … jaraṁ muñcitukamyatā …

byādhiṁ muñcitukamyatā … byādhiṁ muñcitukamyatā …

maraṇaṁ muñcitukamyatā … maraṇaṁ muñcitukamyatā …

sokaṁ muñcitukamyatā … sokaṁ muñcitukamyatā …

paridevaṁ muñcitukamyatā …pe… paridevaṁ muñcitukamyatā …pe…

upāyāsaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. upāyāsaṁ muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Uppādo dukkhanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Pavattaṁ dukkhanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe… muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe…

upāyāso dukkhanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Uppādo bhayanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Pavattaṁ bhayanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe… muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe…

upāyāso bhayanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Uppādo sāmisanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Pavattaṁ sāmisanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe… muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe…

upāyāso sāmisanti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Uppādo saṅkhārāti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Pavattaṁ saṅkhārāti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe… muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ …pe…

upāyāso saṅkhārāti—

muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. muñcitukamyatāpaṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Uppādo saṅkhārā, te saṅkhāre ajjhupekkhatīti—

saṅkhārupekkhā. saṅkhārupekkhā.

Ye ca saṅkhārā yā ca upekkhā ubhopete saṅkhārā, te saṅkhāre ajjhupekkhatīti—Ye ca formations yā ca upekkhā ubhopete saṅkhārā, te saṅkhāre ajjhupekkhatīti—

saṅkhārupekkhā. saṅkhārupekkhā.

Pavattaṁ saṅkhārā …pe… Pavattaṁ formations …pe…

nimittaṁ saṅkhārā … nimittaṁ formations …

āyūhanā saṅkhārā … āyūhanā formations …

paṭisandhi saṅkhārā … paṭisandhi formations …

gati saṅkhārā … gati formations …

nibbatti saṅkhārā … nibbatti formations …

upapatti saṅkhārā … upapatti formations …

jāti saṅkhārā … birth formations …

jarā saṅkhārā … jarā formations …

byādhi saṅkhārā … byādhi formations …

maraṇaṁ saṅkhārā … maraṇaṁ formations …

soko saṅkhārā … soko formations …

paridevo saṅkhārā …pe… paridevo formations …pe…

upāyāso saṅkhārā, te saṅkhāre ajjhupekkhatīti—

saṅkhārupekkhā. saṅkhārupekkhā.

Ye ca saṅkhārā yā ca upekkhā ubhopete saṅkhārā, te saṅkhāre ajjhupekkhatīti—Ye ca formations yā ca upekkhā ubhopete saṅkhārā, te saṅkhāre ajjhupekkhatīti—

saṅkhārupekkhā. saṅkhārupekkhā.

Katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti? Katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti?

Aṭṭhahākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. Aṭṭhahākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti.

Puthujjanassa katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti? Puthujjanassa katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti?

Sekkhassa katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti? Sekkhassa katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti?

Vītarāgassa katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti? Vītarāgassa katihākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti?

Puthujjanassa dvīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. Puthujjanassa dvīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti.

Sekkhassa tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. Sekkhassa tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti.

Vītarāgassa tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. Vītarāgassa tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti.

Puthujjanassa katamehi dvīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti? Puthujjanassa katamehi dvīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti?

Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandati vā vipassati vā. Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandati vā vipassati vā.

Puthujjanassa imehi dvīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. Puthujjanassa imehi dvīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti.

Sekkhassa katamehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti? Sekkhassa katamehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti?

Sekkho saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandati vā vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati. Sekkho saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandati vā vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati.

Sekkhassa imehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. Sekkhassa imehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti.

Vītarāgassa katamehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti? Vītarāgassa katamehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti?

Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati, tadajjhupekkhitvā suññatavihārena vā animittavihārena vā appaṇihitavihārena vā viharati. Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati, tadajjhupekkhitvā suññatavihārena vā animittavihārena vā appaṇihitavihārena vā viharati.

Vītarāgassa imehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti. Vītarāgassa imehi tīhākārehi saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro hoti.

Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti? Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti?

Puthujjanassa saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandato cittaṁ kilissati, bhāvanāya paripantho hoti, paṭivedhassa antarāyo hoti, āyatiṁ paṭisandhiyā paccayo hoti. Puthujjanassa saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandato cittaṁ kilissati, bhāvanāya paripantho hoti, paṭivedhassa antarāyo hoti, āyatiṁ paṭisandhiyā paccayo hoti.

Sekkhassapi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandato cittaṁ kilissati, bhāvanāya paripantho hoti, uttaripaṭivedhassa antarāyo hoti, āyatiṁ paṭisandhiyā paccayo hoti. Sekkhassapi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandato cittaṁ kilissati, bhāvanāya paripantho hoti, uttaripaṭivedhassa antarāyo hoti, āyatiṁ paṭisandhiyā paccayo hoti.

Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti abhinandaṭṭhena. Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti abhinandaṭṭhena.

Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti? Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti?

Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ aniccatopi dukkhatopi anattatopi vipassati. Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ aniccatopi dukkhatopi anattatopi vipassati.

Sekkhopi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ aniccatopi dukkhatopi anattatopi vipassati. Sekkhopi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ aniccatopi dukkhatopi anattatopi vipassati.

Vītarāgopi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ aniccatopi dukkhatopi anattatopi vipassati. Vītarāgopi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ aniccatopi dukkhatopi anattatopi vipassati.

Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti anupassanaṭṭhena. Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro ekattaṁ hoti anupassanaṭṭhena.

Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti? Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti?

Puthujjanassa saṅkhārupekkhā kusalā hoti. Puthujjanassa saṅkhārupekkhā kusalā hoti.

Sekkhassapi saṅkhārupekkhā kusalā hoti. Sekkhassapi saṅkhārupekkhā kusalā hoti.

Vītarāgassa saṅkhārupekkhā abyākatā hoti. Vītarāgassa saṅkhārupekkhā abyākatā hoti.

Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti kusalābyākataṭṭhena. Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti kusalābyākataṭṭhena.

Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti? Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti?

Puthujjanassa saṅkhārupekkhā kiñcikāle suviditā hoti, kiñcikāle na suviditā hoti. Puthujjanassa saṅkhārupekkhā kiñcikāle suviditā hoti, kiñcikāle na suviditā hoti.

Sekkhassapi saṅkhārupekkhā kiñcikāle suviditā hoti, kiñcikāle na suviditā hoti. Sekkhassapi saṅkhārupekkhā kiñcikāle suviditā hoti, kiñcikāle na suviditā hoti.

Vītarāgassa saṅkhārupekkhā accantaṁ suviditā hoti. Vītarāgassa saṅkhārupekkhā accantaṁ suviditā hoti.

Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti viditaṭṭhena ca aviditaṭṭhena ca. Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti viditaṭṭhena ca aviditaṭṭhena ca.

Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti? Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti?

Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ atittattā vipassati. Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ atittattā vipassati.

Sekkhopi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ atittattā vipassati. Sekkhopi saṅkhārupekkhaṁ atittattā vipassati.

Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ tittattā vipassati. Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ tittattā vipassati.

Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti tittaṭṭhena ca atittaṭṭhena ca. Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti tittaṭṭhena ca atittaṭṭhena ca.

Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti? Kathaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti?

Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ tiṇṇaṁ saññojanānaṁ pahānāya sotāpattimaggaṁ paṭilābhatthāya vipassati. Puthujjano saṅkhārupekkhaṁ tiṇṇaṁ saññojanānaṁ pahānāya sotāpattimaggaṁ paṭilābhatthāya vipassati.

Sekkho saṅkhārupekkhaṁ tiṇṇaṁ saññojanānaṁ pahīnattā uttaripaṭilābhatthāya vipassati. Sekkho saṅkhārupekkhaṁ tiṇṇaṁ saññojanānaṁ pahīnattā uttaripaṭilābhatthāya vipassati.

Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ sabbakilesānaṁ pahīnattā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthāya vipassati. Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ sabbakilesānaṁ pahīnattā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāratthāya vipassati.

Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti pahīnaṭṭhena ca appahīnaṭṭhena ca. Evaṁ puthujjanassa ca sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti pahīnaṭṭhena ca appahīnaṭṭhena ca.

Kathaṁ sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti? Kathaṁ sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti?

Sekkho saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandati vā vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati. Sekkho saṅkhārupekkhaṁ abhinandati vā vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati.

Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati, tadajjhupekkhitvā suññatavihārena vā animittavihārena vā appaṇihitavihārena vā viharati. Vītarāgo saṅkhārupekkhaṁ vipassati vā paṭisaṅkhāya vā phalasamāpattiṁ samāpajjati, tadajjhupekkhitvā suññatavihārena vā animittavihārena vā appaṇihitavihārena vā viharati.

Evaṁ sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti vihārasamāpattaṭṭhena. Evaṁ sekkhassa ca vītarāgassa ca saṅkhārupekkhāya cittassa abhinīhāro nānattaṁ hoti vihārasamāpattaṭṭhena.

Kati saṅkhārupekkhā samathavasena uppajjanti? Kati saṅkhārupekkhā samathavasena uppajjanti?

Kati saṅkhārupekkhā vipassanāvasena uppajjanti? Kati saṅkhārupekkhā vipassanāvasena uppajjanti?

Aṭṭha saṅkhārupekkhā samathavasena uppajjanti. Aṭṭha saṅkhārupekkhā samathavasena uppajjanti.

Dasa saṅkhārupekkhā vipassanāvasena uppajjanti. Dasa saṅkhārupekkhā vipassanāvasena uppajjanti.

Katamā aṭṭha saṅkhārupekkhā samathavasena uppajjanti? Katamā aṭṭha saṅkhārupekkhā samathavasena uppajjanti?

Paṭhamaṁ jhānaṁ paṭilābhatthāya nīvaraṇe paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. The first jhana paṭilābhatthāya nīvaraṇe paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Dutiyaṁ jhānaṁ paṭilābhatthāya vitakkavicāre paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. The second jhana paṭilābhatthāya vitakkavicāre paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Tatiyaṁ jhānaṁ paṭilābhatthāya pītiṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. The third jhana paṭilābhatthāya pītiṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Catutthaṁ jhānaṁ paṭilābhatthāya sukhadukkhe paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. The fourth jhana paṭilābhatthāya sukhadukkhe paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Ākāsānañcāyatanasamāpattiṁ paṭilābhatthāya rūpasaññaṁ paṭighasaññaṁ nānattasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. The attainment of the base consisting of boundless spaceṁ paṭilābhatthāya rūpasaññaṁ paṭighasaññaṁ nānattasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Viññāṇañcāyatanasamāpattiṁ paṭilābhatthāya ākāsānañcāyatanasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. consciousnessñcāyatanasamāpattiṁ paṭilābhatthāya ākāsānañcāyatanasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Ākiñcaññāyatanasamāpattiṁ paṭilābhatthāya viññāṇañcāyatanasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. The attainment of the base consisting of nothingnessṁ paṭilābhatthāya consciousnessñcāyatanasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

Nevasaññānāsaññāyatanasamāpattiṁ paṭilābhatthāya ākiñcaññāyatanasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ. The attainment of the base consisting of neither perception nor non-perceptionṁ paṭilābhatthāya ākiñcaññāyatanasaññaṁ paṭisaṅkhāsantiṭṭhanā paññā saṅkhārupekkhāsu ñāṇaṁ.

1.1.11. Maggañāṇaniddesa 1.1.11. Maggañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ dubhato vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭane paññā magge ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ dubhato vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭane paññā magge ñāṇaṁ?

Sotāpattimaggakkhaṇe dassanaṭṭhena sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilesehi ca khandhehi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittehi vuṭṭhāti. Sotāpattimaggakkhaṇe dassanaṭṭhena sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilesehi ca khandhehi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittehi vuṭṭhāti.

Tena vuccati—

“dubhato vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭane paññā magge ñāṇaṁ”. “dubhato vuṭṭhānavivaṭṭane paññā magge ñāṇaṁ”.

jhānaṁ tena pavuccati; jhānaṁ tena pavuccati;

Jhānavimokkhe kusalatā, Jhānavimokkhe kusalatā,

nānādiṭṭhīsu na kampati. nānādiṭṭhīsu na kampati.

Samādahitvā yathā ce vipassati, Samādahitvā yathā ce vipassati,

Vipassamāno tathā ce samādahe; Vipassamāno tathā ce samādahe;

Vipassanā ca samatho tadā ahu, Vipassanā ca samatho tadā ahu,

Samānabhāgā yuganaddhā vattare. Samānabhāgā yuganaddhā vattare.

Dukkhā saṅkhārā sukho, Dukkhā formations sukho,

nirodho iti dassanaṁ; nirodho iti dassanaṁ;

Dubhato vuṭṭhitā paññā, Dubhato vuṭṭhitā paññā,

phasseti amataṁ padaṁ. phasseti amataṁ padaṁ.

1.1.12. Phalañāṇaniddesa 1.1.12. Phalañāṇaniddesa

Sotāpattimaggakkhaṇe dassanaṭṭhena sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilesehi ca khandhehi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittehi vuṭṭhāti. Sotāpattimaggakkhaṇe dassanaṭṭhena sammādiṭṭhi micchādiṭṭhiyā vuṭṭhāti, tadanuvattakakilesehi ca khandhehi ca vuṭṭhāti, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittehi vuṭṭhāti.

Taṁpayogappaṭippassaddhattā uppajjati sammādiṭṭhi. Taṁpayogappaṭippassaddhattā uppajjati sammādiṭṭhi.

Maggassetaṁ phalaṁ. Maggassetaṁ phalaṁ.

1.1.13. Vimuttiñāṇaniddesa 1.1.13. Vimuttiñāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ?

Sotāpattimaggena sakkāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāso, diṭṭhānusayo, vicikicchānusayo attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti. Sotāpattimaggena sakBodydiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāso, diṭṭhānusayo, vicikicchānusayo attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti.

Imehi pañcahi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ. Imehi pañcahi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ.

Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

Tena vuccati—

“chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”. “chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”.

Sakadāgāmimaggena oḷārikaṁ kāmarāgasaññojanaṁ, paṭighasaññojanaṁ, oḷāriko kāmarāgānusayo, paṭighānusayo—

attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti. attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti.

Imehi catūhi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ. Imehi catūhi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ.

Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

Tena vuccati—

“chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”. “chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”.

Anāgāmimaggena anusahagataṁ kāmarāgasaññojanaṁ, paṭighasaññojanaṁ, anusahagato kāmarāgānusayo, paṭighānusayo—

attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti. attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti.

Imehi catūhi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ. Imehi catūhi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ.

Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

Tena vuccati—

“chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”. “chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”.

Arahattamaggena rūparāgo, arūparāgo, māno, uddhaccaṁ, avijjā, mānānusayo, bhavarāgānusayo, avijjānusayo—

attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti. attano cittassa upakkilesā sammā samucchinnā honti.

Imehi aṭṭhahi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ. Imehi aṭṭhahi upakkilesehi sapariyuṭṭhānehi cittaṁ vimuttaṁ hoti suvimuttaṁ.

Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁvimutti ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

“chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”. “chinnavaṭumānupassane paññā vimuttiñāṇaṁ”.

1.1.14 Paccavekkhaṇañāṇaniddesa 1.1.14 Paccavekkhaṇañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ tadā samudāgate dhamme passane paññā paccavekkhaṇe ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ tadā samudāgate dhamme passane paññā paccavekkhaṇe ñāṇaṁ?

Sotāpattimaggakkhaṇe dassanaṭṭhena sammādiṭṭhi tadā samudāgatā. Sotāpattimaggakkhaṇe dassanaṭṭhena sammādiṭṭhi tadā samudāgatā.

1.1.15. Vatthunānattañāṇaniddesa 1.1.15. Vatthunānattañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ ajjhattavavatthāne paññā vatthunānatte ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ ajjhattavavatthāne paññā vatthunānatte ñāṇaṁ?

Kathaṁ ajjhattadhamme vavattheti? Kathaṁ ajjhattadhamme vavattheti?

Cakkhuṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, sotaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, ghānaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, jivhaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, kāyaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, manaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti. Eyeṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, Ear ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, Noseṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, jivhaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, Bodyṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti, manaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti.

Kathaṁ cakkhuṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti? Kathaṁ Eyeṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti?

Cakkhu avijjāsambhūtanti vavattheti, cakkhu taṇhāsambhūtanti vavattheti, cakkhu kammasambhūtanti vavattheti, cakkhu āhārasambhūtanti vavattheti, cakkhu catunnaṁ mahābhūtānaṁ upādāyāti vavattheti, cakkhu uppannanti vavattheti, cakkhu samudāgatanti vavattheti. Eye avijjāsambhūtanti vavattheti, Eye taṇhāsambhūtanti vavattheti, Eye kammasambhūtanti vavattheti, Eye āhārasambhūtanti vavattheti, Eye catunnaṁ mahābhūtānaṁ upādāyāti vavattheti, Eye uppannanti vavattheti, Eye samudāgatanti vavattheti.

Cakkhu ahutvā sambhūtaṁ, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti. Eye ahutvā sambhūtaṁ, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti.

Cakkhuṁ antavantato vavattheti, cakkhu addhuvaṁ asassataṁ vipariṇāmadhammanti vavattheti, cakkhu aniccaṁ saṅkhataṁ paṭiccasamuppannaṁ khayadhammaṁ vayadhammaṁ virāgadhammaṁ nirodhadhammanti vavattheti. Eyeṁ antavantato vavattheti, Eye addhuvaṁ asassataṁ vipariṇāmadhammanti vavattheti, Eye aniccaṁ saṅkhataṁ paṭiccasamuppannaṁ khayadhammaṁ vayadhammaṁ virāgadhammaṁ nirodhadhammanti vavattheti.

Cakkhuṁ aniccato vavattheti, no niccato; Eyeṁ aniccato vavattheti, no niccato;

dukkhato vavattheti, no sukhato; dukkhato vavattheti, no sukhato;

anattato vavattheti, no attato; anattato vavattheti, no attato;

nibbindati, no nandati; nibbindati, no nandati;

virajjati, no rajjati; virajjati, no rajjati;

nirodheti, no samudeti; nirodheti, no samudeti;

paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati. paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati.

Aniccato vavatthento niccasaññaṁ pajahati, dukkhato vavatthento sukhasaññaṁ pajahati, anattato vavatthento attasaññaṁ pajahati, nibbindanto nandiṁ pajahati, virajjanto rāgaṁ pajahati, nirodhento samudayaṁ pajahati, paṭinissajjanto ādānaṁ pajahati. Aniccato vavatthento niccasaññaṁ pajahati, dukkhato vavatthento sukhasaññaṁ pajahati, anattato vavatthento attasaññaṁ pajahati, nibbindanto nandiṁ pajahati, virajjanto rāgaṁ pajahati, nirodhento samudayaṁ pajahati, paṭinissajjanto ādānaṁ pajahati.

Evaṁ cakkhuṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti. Evaṁ Eyeṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti.

Kathaṁ sotaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti? Kathaṁ Ear ajjhattaṁ vavattheti?

Sotaṁ avijjāsambhūtanti vavattheti …pe… Ear avijjāsambhūtanti vavattheti …pe…

evaṁ sotaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti. evaṁ Ear ajjhattaṁ vavattheti.

Kathaṁ ghānaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti? Kathaṁ Noseṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti?

Ghānaṁ avijjāsambhūtanti vavattheti …pe… Noseṁ avijjāsambhūtanti vavattheti …pe…

evaṁ ghānaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti. evaṁ Noseṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti.

Kathaṁ jivhaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti? Kathaṁ jivhaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti?

Jivhā avijjāsambhūtāti vavattheti, jivhā taṇhāsambhūtāti vavattheti, jivhā kammasambhūtāti vavattheti, jivhā āhārasambhūtāti vavattheti, jivhā catunnaṁ mahābhūtānaṁ upādāyāti vavattheti, jivhā uppannāti vavattheti, jivhā samudāgatāti vavattheti. Tongue avijjāsambhūtāti vavattheti, Tongue taṇhāsambhūtāti vavattheti, Tongue kammasambhūtāti vavattheti, Tongue āhārasambhūtāti vavattheti, Tongue catunnaṁ mahābhūtānaṁ upādāyāti vavattheti, Tongue uppannāti vavattheti, Tongue samudāgatāti vavattheti.

Jivhā ahutvā sambhūtā, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti. Tongue ahutvā sambhūtā, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti.

Jivhaṁ antavantato vavattheti, jivhā addhuvā asassatā vipariṇāmadhammāti vavattheti, jivhā aniccā saṅkhatā paṭiccasamuppannā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā nirodhadhammāti vavattheti. Jivhaṁ antavantato vavattheti, Tongue addhuvā asassatā vipariṇāmadhammāti vavattheti, Tongue aniccā saṅkhatā paṭiccasamuppannā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā nirodhadhammāti vavattheti.

Jivhaṁ aniccato vavattheti, no niccato …pe… Jivhaṁ aniccato vavattheti, no niccato …pe…

paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati. paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati.

Aniccato vavatthento niccasaññaṁ pajahati …pe… Aniccato vavatthento niccasaññaṁ pajahati …pe…

paṭinissajjanto ādānaṁ pajahati. paṭinissajjanto ādānaṁ pajahati.

Evaṁ jivhaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti. Evaṁ jivhaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti.

Kathaṁ kāyaṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti? Kathaṁ Bodyṁ ajjhattaṁ vavattheti?

Kāyo avijjāsambhūtoti vavattheti, kāyo taṇhāsambhūtoti vavattheti, kāyo kammasambhūtoti vavattheti, kāyo āhārasambhūtoti vavattheti, kāyo catunnaṁ mahābhūtānaṁ upādāyāti vavattheti, kāyo uppannoti vavattheti, kāyo samudāgatoti vavattheti. Body avijjāsambhūtoti vavattheti, Body taṇhāsambhūtoti vavattheti, Body kammasambhūtoti vavattheti, Body āhārasambhūtoti vavattheti, Body catunnaṁ mahābhūtānaṁ upādāyāti vavattheti, Body uppannoti vavattheti, Body samudāgatoti vavattheti.

Kāyo ahutvā sambhūto, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti. Body ahutvā sambhūto, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti.

Kāyaṁ antavantato vavattheti, kāyo addhuvo asassato vipariṇāmadhammoti vavattheti, kāyo anicco saṅkhato paṭiccasamuppanno khayadhammo vayadhammo virāgadhammo nirodhadhammoti vavattheti. Mano ahutvā sambhūto, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti. Mind ahutvā sambhūto, hutvā na bhavissatīti vavattheti.

nibbindati, no nandati; nibbindati, no nandati;

virajjati, no rajjati; virajjati, no rajjati;

nirodheti, no samudeti; nirodheti, no samudeti;

paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati. paṭinissajjati, no ādiyati.

1.1.17. Cariyānānattañāṇaniddesa 1.1.17. Cariyānānattañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ cariyāvavatthāne paññā cariyānānatte ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ cariyāvavatthāne paññā cariyānānatte ñāṇaṁ?

Cariyāti tisso cariyāyo—Cariyāti tisso cariyāyo—

viññāṇacariyā, aññāṇacariyā, ñāṇacariyā. consciousnesscariyā, aññāṇacariyā, ñāṇacariyā.

Katamā viññāṇacariyā? Katamā consciousnesscariyā?

Dassanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā viññāṇacariyā rūpesu, dassanaṭṭho cakkhuviññāṇaṁ viññāṇacariyā rūpesu, diṭṭhattā abhiniropanā vipākamanodhātu viññāṇacariyā rūpesu, abhiniropitattā vipākamanoviññāṇadhātu viññāṇacariyā rūpesu. Dassanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā consciousnesscariyā rūpesu, dassanaṭṭho Eyeconsciousness consciousnesscariyā rūpesu, diṭṭhattā abhiniropanā vipākaMind principle consciousnesscariyā rūpesu, abhiniropitattā vipākaMindconsciousness principle consciousnesscariyā rūpesu.

Savanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā viññāṇacariyā saddesu, savanattho sotaviññāṇaṁ viññāṇacariyā saddesu, sutattā abhiniropanā vipākamanodhātu viññāṇacariyā saddesu, abhiniropitattā vipākamanoviññāṇadhātu viññāṇacariyā saddesu. Savanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā consciousnesscariyā saddesu, savanattho Ear-consciousness consciousnesscariyā saddesu, sutattā abhiniropanā vipākaMind principle consciousnesscariyā saddesu, abhiniropitattā vipākaMindconsciousness principle consciousnesscariyā saddesu.

Ghāyanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā viññāṇacariyā gandhesu, ghāyanaṭṭho ghānaviññāṇaṁ viññāṇacariyā gandhesu, ghāyitattā abhiniropanā vipākamanodhātu viññāṇacariyā gandhesu, abhiniropitattā vipākamanoviññāṇadhātu viññāṇacariyā gandhesu. Ghāyanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā consciousnesscariyā gandhesu, ghāyanaṭṭho Noseconsciousness consciousnesscariyā gandhesu, ghāyitattā abhiniropanā vipākaMind principle consciousnesscariyā gandhesu, abhiniropitattā vipākaMindconsciousness principle consciousnesscariyā gandhesu.

Sāyanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā viññāṇacariyā rasesu, sāyanaṭṭho jivhāviññāṇaṁ viññāṇacariyā rasesu, sāyitattā abhiniropanā vipākamanodhātu viññāṇacariyā rasesu, abhiniropitattā vipākamanoviññāṇadhātu viññāṇacariyā rasesu. Phusanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā consciousnesscariyā phoṭṭhabbesu, phusanaṭṭho Bodyconsciousness consciousnesscariyā phoṭṭhabbesu, phuṭṭhattā abhiniropanā vipākaMind principle consciousnesscariyā phoṭṭhabbesu, abhiniropitattā vipākaMindconsciousness principle consciousnesscariyā phoṭṭhabbesu.

Vijānanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā viññāṇacariyā dhammesu, vijānanaṭṭho manoviññāṇaṁ viññāṇacariyā dhammesu. Vijānanatthāya āvajjanakiriyābyākatā consciousnesscariyā dhammesu, vijānanaṭṭho Mindconsciousness consciousnesscariyā dhammesu.

1.1.18. Bhūminānattañāṇaniddesa 1.1.18. Bhūminānattañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ catudhammavavatthāne paññā bhūminānatte ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ catudhammavavatthāne paññā bhūminānatte ñāṇaṁ?

Catasso bhūmiyo—

kāmāvacarā bhūmi, rūpāvacarā bhūmi, arūpāvacarā bhūmi, apariyāpannā bhūmi. kāmāvacarā bhūmi, rūpāvacarā bhūmi, arūpāvacarā bhūmi, apariyāpannā bhūmi.

Katamā kāmāvacarā bhūmi? Katamā kāmāvacarā bhūmi?

Heṭṭhato avīcinirayaṁ pariyantaṁ karitvā uparito paranimmitavasavattī deve antokaritvā yaṁ etasmiṁ antare etthāvacarā ettha pariyāpannā khandhadhātuāyatanā rūpaṁ vedanā saññā saṅkhārā viññāṇaṁ—Heṭṭhato avīcinirayaṁ pariyantaṁ karitvā uparito paranimmitavasavattī deve antokaritvā yaṁ etasmiṁ antare etthāvacarā ettha pariyāpannā khandha principleāyatanā materiality feeling saññā formations consciousness—

ayaṁ kāmāvacarā bhūmi. ayaṁ kāmāvacarā bhūmi.

Katamā rūpāvacarā bhūmi? Katamā rūpāvacarā bhūmi?

Heṭṭhato brahmalokaṁ pariyantaṁ karitvā uparito akaniṭṭhe deve antokaritvā yaṁ etasmiṁ antare etthāvacarā ettha pariyāpannā samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā—

ayaṁ rūpāvacarā bhūmi. ayaṁ rūpāvacarā bhūmi.

Katamā arūpāvacarā bhūmi? Katamā arūpāvacarā bhūmi?

Heṭṭhato ākāsānañcāyatanūpage deve pariyantaṁ karitvā uparito nevasaññānāsaññāyatanūpage deve antokaritvā yaṁ etasmiṁ antare etthāvacarā ettha pariyāpannā samāpannassa vā upapannassa vā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārissa vā cittacetasikā dhammā—

ayaṁ arūpāvacarā bhūmi. ayaṁ arūpāvacarā bhūmi.

Katamā apariyāpannā bhūmi? Katamā apariyāpannā bhūmi?

Apariyāpannā maggā ca maggaphalāni ca asaṅkhatā ca dhātu—Apariyāpannā maggā ca maggaphalāni ca asaṅkhatā ca principle—

ayaṁ apariyāpannā bhūmi. ayaṁ apariyāpannā bhūmi.

Imā catasso bhūmiyo. Imā catasso bhūmiyo.

Aparāpi catasso bhūmiyo cattāro satipaṭṭhānā cattāro sammappadhānā, cattāro iddhipādā, cattāri jhānāni, catasso appamaññāyo, catasso arūpasamāpattiyo, catasso paṭisambhidā, catasso paṭipadā, cattāri ārammaṇāni, cattāro ariyavaṁsā, cattāri saṅgahavatthūni, cattāri cakkāni, cattāri dhammapadāni—

imā catasso bhūmiyo. imā catasso bhūmiyo.

Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

Tena vuccati—

“catudhammavavatthāne paññā bhūminānatte ñāṇaṁ”. “catudhammavavatthāne paññā bhūminānatte ñāṇaṁ”.

Bhūminānattañāṇaniddeso aṭṭhārasamo. Bhūminānattañāṇaniddeso aṭṭhārasamo.

1.1.19. Dhammanānattañāṇaniddesa 1.1.19. Dhammanānattañāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ navadhammavavatthāne paññā dhammanānatte ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ navadhammavavatthāne paññā dhammanānatte ñāṇaṁ?

Kathaṁ dhamme vavattheti? Kathaṁ dhamme vavattheti?

Kāmāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, akusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. Kāmāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, akusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Rūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. Rūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Arūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. Arūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Apariyāpanne dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. Apariyāpanne dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Kathaṁ kāmāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, akusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti? Kathaṁ kāmāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, akusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti?

Dasa kusalakammapathe kusalato vavattheti, dasa akusalakammapathe akusalato vavattheti, rūpañca vipākañca kiriyañca abyākatato vavattheti—

evaṁ kāmāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, akusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. evaṁ kāmāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, akusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Kathaṁ rūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti? Kathaṁ rūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti?

Idhaṭṭhassa cattāri jhānāni kusalato vavattheti, tatrūpapannassa cattāri jhānāni abyākatato vavattheti—

evaṁ rūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. evaṁ rūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Kathaṁ arūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti? Kathaṁ arūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti?

Idhaṭṭhassa catasso arūpāvacarasamāpattiyo kusalato vavattheti, tatrūpapannassa catasso arūpāvacarasamāpattiyo abyākatato vavattheti—

evaṁ arūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. evaṁ arūpāvacare dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Kathaṁ apariyāpanne dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti? Kathaṁ apariyāpanne dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti?

Cattāro ariyamagge kusalato vavattheti, cattāri ca sāmaññaphalāni nibbānañca abyākatato vavattheti—

evaṁ apariyāpanne dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti. evaṁ apariyāpanne dhamme kusalato vavattheti, abyākatato vavattheti.

Evaṁ dhamme vavattheti. Evaṁ dhamme vavattheti.

Nava pāmojjamūlakā dhammā. Nava pāmojjamūlakā dhammā.

Aniccato manasikaroto pāmojjaṁ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, pītimanassa kāyo passambhati, passaddhakāyo sukhaṁ vedeti, sukhino cittaṁ samādhiyati. 1.1.20–24 Ñāṇapañcakaniddesa 1.1.20–24 Ñāṇapañcakaniddesa

Kathaṁ abhiññāpaññā ñātaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pariññāpaññā tīraṇaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pahāne paññā pariccāgaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, bhāvanā paññā ekarasaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, sacchikiriyāpaññā phassanaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ abhiññāpaññā ñātaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pariññāpaññā tīraṇaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pahāne paññā pariccāgaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, bhāvanā paññā ekarasaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, sacchikiriyāpaññā phassanaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ?

Ye ye dhammā abhiññātā honti, te te dhammā ñātā honti. Ye ye dhammā abhiññātā honti, te te dhammā ñātā honti.

Ye ye dhammā pariññātā honti, te te dhammā tīritā honti. Ye ye dhammā pariññātā honti, te te dhammā tīritā honti.

Ye ye dhammā pahīnā honti, te te dhammā pariccattā honti. Ye ye dhammā pahīnā honti, te te dhammā pariccattā honti.

Ye ye dhammā bhāvitā honti, te te dhammā ekarasā honti. Ye ye dhammā bhāvitā honti, te te dhammā ekaFlavours honti.

Ye ye dhammā sacchikatā honti, te te dhammā phassitā honti. Ye ye dhammā sacchikatā honti, te te dhammā phassitā honti.

Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā. Taṁ ñātaṭṭhena ñāṇaṁ, pajānanaṭṭhena paññā.

Tena vuccati—

“abhiññā paññā ñātaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pariññā paññā tīraṇaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pahāne paññā pariccāgaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, bhāvanā paññā ekarasaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, sacchikiriyā paññā phassanaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ”. “abhiññā paññā ñātaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pariññā paññā tīraṇaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, pahāne paññā pariccāgaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, bhāvanā paññā ekarasaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ, sacchikiriyā paññā phassanaṭṭhe ñāṇaṁ”.

Ñāṇapañcakaniddeso catuvīsatimo. Ñāṇapañcakaniddeso catuvīsatimo.

1.1.25–28 Paṭisambhidāñāṇaniddesa 1.1.25–28 Paṭisambhidāñāṇaniddesa

Kathaṁ atthanānatte paññā atthapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ, dhammanānatte paññā dhammapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ, niruttinānatte paññā niruttipaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ, paṭibhānanānatte paññā paṭibhānapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ? Kathaṁ atthanānatte paññā atthapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ, dhammanānatte paññā dhammapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ, niruttinānatte paññā niruttipaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ, paṭibhānanānatte paññā paṭibhānapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ?

Saddhindriyaṁ dhammo, vīriyindriyaṁ dhammo, satindriyaṁ dhammo, samādhindriyaṁ dhammo, paññindriyaṁ dhammo. Saddhindriyaṁ dhammo, vīriyindriyaṁ dhammo, satindriyaṁ dhammo, samādhindriyaṁ dhammo, paññindriyaṁ dhammo.

Añño saddhindriyaṁ dhammo, añño vīriyindriyaṁ dhammo, añño satindriyaṁ dhammo, añño samādhindriyaṁ dhammo, añño paññindriyaṁ dhammo. Añño saddhindriyaṁ dhammo, añño vīriyindriyaṁ dhammo, añño satindriyaṁ dhammo, añño samādhindriyaṁ dhammo, añño paññindriyaṁ dhammo.

Yena ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā ñātā, teneva ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā paṭividitāti. Yena ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā ñātā, teneva ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā paṭividitāti.

Tena vuccati—

“dhammanānatte paññā dhammapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ”.

Adhimokkhaṭṭho attho, paggahaṭṭho attho, upaṭṭhānaṭṭho attho, avikkhepaṭṭho attho, dassanaṭṭho attho. Adhimokkhaṭṭho attho, paggahaṭṭho attho, upaṭṭhānaṭṭho attho, avikkhepaṭṭho attho, dassanaṭṭho attho.

Añño adhimokkhaṭṭho attho, añño paggahaṭṭho attho, añño upaṭṭhānaṭṭho attho, añño avikkhepaṭṭho attho, añño dassanaṭṭho attho. Añño adhimokkhaṭṭho attho, añño paggahaṭṭho attho, añño upaṭṭhānaṭṭho attho, añño avikkhepaṭṭho attho, añño dassanaṭṭho attho.

Yena ñāṇena ime nānā atthā ñātā, teneva ñāṇena ime nānā atthā paṭividitāti. Yena ñāṇena ime nānā atthā ñātā, teneva ñāṇena ime nānā atthā paṭividitāti.

Tena vuccati “atthanānatte paññā atthapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ”.

Pañca dhamme sandassetuṁ byañjananiruttābhilāpā, pañca atthe sandassetuṁ byañjananiruttābhilāpā. Pañca dhamme sandassetuṁ byañjananiruttābhilāpā, pañca atthe sandassetuṁ byañjananiruttābhilāpā.

Aññā dhammaniruttiyo, aññā atthaniruttiyo. Aññā dhammaniruttiyo, aññā atthaniruttiyo.

Yena ñāṇena imā nānā niruttiyo ñātā, teneva ñāṇena imā nānā niruttiyo paṭividitāti. Yena ñāṇena imā nānā niruttiyo ñātā, teneva ñāṇena imā nānā niruttiyo paṭividitāti.

Tena vuccati—

“niruttinānatte paññā niruttipaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ”.

Pañcasu dhammesu ñāṇāni, pañcasu atthesu ñāṇāni, dasasu niruttīsu ñāṇāni. Pañcasu dhammesu ñāṇāni, pañcasu atthesu ñāṇāni, dasasu niruttīsu ñāṇāni.

Aññāni dhammesu ñāṇāni, aññāni atthesu ñāṇāni, aññāni niruttīsu ñāṇāni. Aññāni dhammesu ñāṇāni, aññāni atthesu ñāṇāni, aññāni niruttīsu ñāṇāni.

Tena vuccati—

Añño satisambojjhaṅgo dhammo, añño dhammavicayasambojjhaṅgo dhammo, añño vīriyasambojjhaṅgo dhammo, añño pītisambojjhaṅgo dhammo, añño passaddhisambojjhaṅgo dhammo, añño samādhisambojjhaṅgo dhammo, añño upekkhāsambojjhaṅgo dhammo. Yena ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā ñātā, teneva ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā paṭividitāti. Yena ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā ñātā, teneva ñāṇena ime nānā dhammā paṭividitāti.

Tena vuccati—

“dhammanānatte paññā dhammapaṭisambhide ñāṇaṁ”.

Upaṭṭhānaṭṭho attho, pavicayaṭṭho attho, paggahaṭṭho attho, pharaṇaṭṭho attho, upasamaṭṭho attho, avikkhepaṭṭho attho, paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭho attho. Upaṭṭhānaṭṭho attho, pavicayaṭṭho attho, paggahaṭṭho attho, pharaṇaṭṭho attho, upasamaṭṭho attho, avikkhepaṭṭho attho, paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭho attho.

Añño upaṭṭhānaṭṭho attho, añño pavicayaṭṭho attho, añño paggahaṭṭho attho, añño pharaṇaṭṭho attho, añño upasamaṭṭho attho, añño avikkhepaṭṭho attho, añño paṭisaṅkhānaṭṭho attho.
